  values: Uint8Array;
  settled: boolean;
  netOfPin: ReadonlyMap<string, NetId>;
  ordinalOf: ReadonlyMap<NetId, number>;
  /** Bumped on every simulation pass, for anything that wants a coarse signal. */
  tick: number;
}

export const simStore = createStore<SimStoreState>(() => ({
  values: new Uint8Array(0),
  settled: true,
  netOfPin: new Map(),
  ordinalOf: new Map(),
  tick: 0,
}));

/** The value on a pin. Subscribes to that pin's net ONLY. */
export function useNetValue(ref: PinRef | null): Logic {
  return useEndpointValue(ref ? pinEnd(ref) : null);
}

/**
 * The value on any endpoint — a pin, or a hole on the breadboard. One lookup
 * serves both, because endpointKey() on a pin IS pinKey().
 */
export function useEndpointValue(end: Endpoint | null): Logic {
  return useStore(simStore, (s) => {
    if (!end) return LZ;
    const net = s.netOfPin.get(endpointKey(end));
    if (net === undefined) return LZ;
    const ordinal = s.ordinalOf.get(net);
    if (ordinal === undefined) return LZ;
    return (s.values[ordinal] ?? LZ) as Logic;
  });
}

/** Read a pin's value without subscribing — for event handlers and rAF loops. */
export function peekNetValue(ref: PinRef): Logic {
  const s = simStore.getState();
  const net = s.netOfPin.get(endpointKey(pinEnd(ref)));
  if (net === undefined) return LZ;
  const ordinal = s.ordinalOf.get(net);
  if (ordinal === undefined) return LZ;
  return (s.values[ordinal] ?? LZ) as Logic;
}

/** Distinct hues, cycled by net ordinal. */
const WIRE_COLORS = 8;

export interface WirePaint {
  /** Stroke colour. */
  readonly color: string;
  /** Draw the bright halo under the core. */
  readonly glow: boolean;
  /** Fade it back — a LOW wire is real, but it is not the one carrying signal. */
  readonly dim: boolean;
  /** Dash it — a floating net is not a connection you can rely on. */
  readonly dashed: boolean;
}

/**
 * HOW A WIRE IS PAINTED, and the rule that governs it.
 *
 * Colouring purely by logic level made every LOW wire the same dark slate — and
 * on a dark canvas they simply vanished. Fourteen wires were on screen and you
 * could see four. Worse, you could not trace a connection: every HIGH wire was
 * the same green.
 *
 * So a wire takes the colour of its NET, cycled through eight distinct hues —
 * which is exactly why real jumper wire is multicoloured, and for exactly the
 * same reason: so you can follow one connection across a crowded board.
 *
 * BUT THE FAULT COLOURS ARE NOT NEGOTIABLE. A floating (Z) or conflicting (X)
 * net keeps its own unmistakable colour and its own dashing, because a wire that
 * is broken must never be able to look like a wire that is working. Net identity
 * is a convenience; Z and X are the product.
 */
export function wirePaint(value: Logic, netOrdinal: number | null): WirePaint {
  if (value === 2) {
    return { color: "var(--logic-z)", glow: false, dim: false, dashed: true };
  }
  if (value === 3) {
    return { color: "var(--logic-x)", glow: false, dim: false, dashed: false };
  }

  const hue = netOrdinal === null ? 1 : (netOrdinal % WIRE_COLORS) + 1;
  return {
    color: `var(--wire-${hue})`,
    glow: value === 1,
    dim: value === 0,
    dashed: false,
  };
}

/** The ordinal of the net an endpoint sits on — its stable colour index. */
export function useNetOrdinal(end: Endpoint | null): number | null {
  return useStore(simStore, (s) => {
    if (!end) return null;
    const net = s.netOfPin.get(endpointKey(end));
    if (net === undefined) return null;
    return s.ordinalOf.get(net) ?? null;
  });
}

/** The CSS custom property for a logic level. Raw --logic-* token, not --color-*. */
export function logicColor(v: Logic): string {
  switch (v) {
    case 1:
      return "var(--logic-high)";
    case 0:
      return "var(--logic-low)";
    case 2:
      return "var(--logic-z)";
    default:
      return "var(--logic-x)";
  }
}


=== FILE: src/stores/circuit-store.ts ===
"use client";

import { create } from "zustand";
import { persist } from "zustand/middleware";
import { elaborate } from "@/lib/simulation/elaborate";
import {
  evaluate,
  fromSeqState,
  toSeqState,
  type StoredState,
} from "@/lib/simulation/solver";
import { diagnose, type Diagnostic } from "@/lib/simulation/diagnostics";
import {
  asNodeId,
  asWireId,
  emptyDocument,
  endpointKey,
  holeEnd,
  pinEnd,
  pinKey,
  type CircuitDocument,
  type CircuitNode,
  type NetIndex,
  type NodeDraft as SharedDraft,
  type NodeId,
  type PinRef,
  type Point,
  type Wire,
  type WireId,
} from "@/lib/simulation/netlist";
import type { GateOp } from "@/lib/simulation/logic";
import { arityOf, gateInputNames } from "@/lib/simulation/parts";
import { routeAll, type Route } from "@/lib/simulation/router";
import { placeOnBreadboard, seatOnBoard } from "@/lib/simulation/place";
import {
  DEFAULT_BOARD,
  dipHoles,
  holeKey,
  isPositiveRail,
  stripOf,
  type BreadboardSpec,
  type HoleRef,
} from "@/lib/simulation/breadboard";
import { getIc } from "@/lib/simulation/ic-library";
import { simStore } from "./sim-store";

/**
 * The circuit DOCUMENT store — topology and geometry. Changes only when the user
 * edits, which is rare compared to simulation ticks.
 *
 * Net values live in a separate store (sim-store) precisely so that flipping one
 * switch does not re-render all 400 SVG nodes. See the note there.
 *
 * Undo/redo is a plain snapshot stack. Circuits are kilobytes; immer patches or
 * zundo would be machinery we do not need.
 */

/** A node draft with the label optional too — the store invents one if omitted. */
export type NewNode = SharedDraft extends infer T
  ? T extends SharedDraft
    ? Omit<T, "label"> & { label?: string }
    : never
  : never;

interface CircuitState {
  doc: CircuitDocument;
  selection: readonly NodeId[];
  /** Pin the user clicked first, while drawing a wire. */
  pendingPin: PinRef | null;
  index: NetIndex | null;
  diagnostics: readonly Diagnostic[];
  /**
   * Wire id -> its routed path, or null when no route exists.
   *
   * DERIVED, exactly like the nets — and deliberately NOT stored in the document.
   * A route is cosmetic: it must never enter undo history (nobody wants to undo a
   * bend), and connectivity must never depend on it. A wire that fails to route is
   * still a wire; the canvas just draws it straight.
   */
  routes: ReadonlyMap<string, Route | null>;

  past: CircuitDocument[];
  future: CircuitDocument[];

  /** How many inputs the next gate dropped from the palette gets (2, 3 or 4). */
  gateInputs: number;
  setGateInputs: (n: number) => void;

  /**
   * Add a part. `null` means the breadboard had no room for it — the caller must
   * say so, because the alternative is a part that exists in the netlist and
   * cannot be seen anywhere on the board.
   */
  addNode: (node: NewNode) => NodeId | null;
  moveNode: (id: NodeId, pos: Point) => void;
  /** Change how many inputs a gate has. Ignored for NOT/BUF (always 1). */
  setGateArity: (id: NodeId, arity: number) => void;
  deleteSelected: () => void;
  select: (ids: readonly NodeId[]) => void;
  toggleSwitch: (id: NodeId) => void;

  /**
   * What every flip-flop is storing, keyed by chip and slot.
   *
   * In the store rather than recomputed because it is the one thing a circuit
   * knows that its document does not — the whole difference between a gate and
   * a flip-flop.
   */
  flopState: StoredState;
  /** Drive one clock node to a new level. Not an edit (does not pollute undo). */
  setClock: (id: NodeId, to: (level: 0 | 1) => 0 | 1) => void;
  /** Toggle a clock once: one edge. */
  toggleClock: (id: NodeId) => void;
  /** Low, high, low — one complete pulse, so both edges happen. In UI, flashes high for ~120ms. */
  pulseClock: (id: NodeId, sync?: boolean) => void;
  /** Synchronous low, high, low pulse without timers. */
  pulseClockSync: (id: NodeId) => void;
  /** Forget every stored bit, as though the power had just come on. */
  resetState: () => void;

  clickPin: (ref: PinRef) => void;
  /** The breadboard equivalent: click one hole, then another, to lay a jumper. */
  clickHole: (hole: HoleRef) => void;
  pendingHole: HoleRef | null;
  cancelWire: () => void;
  deleteWire: (id: WireId) => void;
  setWireColor: (id: WireId, color?: string) => void;
  activeWireColor: string | null;
  setActiveWireColor: (color: string | null) => void;
  setLedColor: (id: NodeId, color: string) => void;
  rotateNode: (id: NodeId, delta?: 90 | -90) => void;
  flipNode: (id: NodeId, axis: "x" | "y") => void;
  autoWireIcPower: (nodeId: string) => void;
  /**
   * The SCHEMATIC this board was seated from, kept so the view can switch back.
   *
   * Going to the breadboard is a *realization*, and it throws information away —
   * the schematic's layout has no counterpart on a board. So rather than try to
   * reverse it (which would have to invent a layout the circuit never had), we
   * simply keep the original and hand it back.
   */
  schematic: CircuitDocument | null;
  /** Re-seat the current schematic onto a real breadboard. */
  toBreadboard: (spec?: BreadboardSpec) => readonly string[];
  /** Return to the schematic this board came from. */
  toSchematic: () => boolean;

  /**
   * The expression this board was SYNTHESIZED from, or null if a human wired it.
   *
   * This is what makes it safe to re-synthesize when the gate rule changes. Silently
   * rewriting a circuit somebody built by hand is unforgivable — but a circuit the
   * app generated is not their work, it is the app's answer to a question, and when
   * the question changes ("NAND only now") the answer must change with it.
   *
   * So provenance is not a nicety here: it is exactly the line between "obey the new
   * rule" and "destroy the user's board".
   */
  origin: string | null;

  load: (doc: CircuitDocument, origin?: string | null) => void;
  clear: () => void;
  undo: () => void;
  redo: () => void;
}

let counter = 0;
const nextId = (prefix: string): string => `${prefix}${(counter += 1)}`;

/** Default labels: switches get A, B, C…; everything else gets a typed serial. */
function defaultLabel(doc: CircuitDocument, node: NewNode): string {
  const existing = Object.values(doc.nodes);

  if (node.kind === "switch") {
    const used = new Set(existing.filter((n) => n.kind === "switch").map((n) => n.label));
    for (let i = 0; i < 26; i++) {
      const name = String.fromCharCode(65 + i);
      if (!used.has(name)) return name;
    }
    return nextId("SW");
  }

  if (node.kind === "led") {
    const n = existing.filter((x) => x.kind === "led").length;
    return n === 0 ? "Q" : `Q${n}`;
  }

  const prefix =
    node.kind === "ic" ? "U" : node.kind === "rail" ? (node.rail === "vcc" ? "V" : "GND") : "G";
  const n = existing.filter((x) => x.kind === node.kind).length + 1;
  return `${prefix}${n}`;
}

/**
 * Rebuild the nets and re-run the simulation.
 *
 * Always a full rebuild — never an in-place net patch (Invariant 1). It is
 * sub-millisecond, and incremental net surgery is where ghost connections come
 * from.
 */
function simulate(
  doc: CircuitDocument,
  stored?: StoredState,
): {
  index: NetIndex;
  diagnostics: Diagnostic[];
  routes: ReadonlyMap<string, Route | null>;
  flopState: StoredState;
} {
  const { index, netlist } = elaborate(doc);

  // Switch states live in the document, so the input vector is read from it
  // rather than held separately — one source of truth.
  const inputs = netlist.inputs.map((port) => {
    const node = doc.nodes[port.node];
    return node?.kind === "switch" ? node.state : 0;
  });

  // Sequential state is carried IN and back OUT. A flip-flop is the one thing
  // here whose output is not a function of the document alone.
  const state = evaluate(netlist, inputs, { state: toSeqState(netlist, stored) });
  const diagnostics = diagnose(doc, index, netlist, state);

  // Push values into the high-frequency store. React does not subscribe to this
  // broadly — the canvas paints from it per-net.
  simStore.setState({
    values: state.values,
    settled: state.settled,
    netOfPin: index.netOfPin,
    ordinalOf: index.ordinalOf,
    tick: simStore.getState().tick + 1,
  });

  // Routing is a SCHEMATIC concern. On a breadboard a jumper is a physical wire
  // between two holes — it arcs over everything, it does not route around
  // obstacles, and pretending otherwise would be drawing a schematic on top of a
  // photograph of a board.
  const routes = doc.board
    ? new Map<string, Route | null>()
    : routeAll(doc, (wire) => {
        const netId = index.netOfEndpoint.get(endpointKey(wire.a));
        return netId ? (index.ordinalOf.get(netId) ?? 0) + 1 : 0;
      }).paths;

  return { index, diagnostics, routes, flopState: fromSeqState(netlist, state.state) };
}

export const useCircuitStore = create<CircuitState>()(
  persist(
    (set, get) => {
  /**
   * Commit a topology change: push undo, re-simulate, clear redo.
   *
   * And DROP THE PROVENANCE. The moment a human adds a gate or cuts a wire, this is
   * no longer the circuit the synthesizer produced, and re-synthesizing it on a rule
   * change would throw their work away. `keepOrigin` is for the edits that change no
   * topology at all — dragging a chip, flipping a switch — where the board is still
   * exactly what was built.
   */
  let pulseTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;

  /** Drive one clock node to a new level and re-simulate. Not an edit. */
  const setClock = (id: NodeId, to: (level: 0 | 1) => 0 | 1): void => {
    const { doc, flopState } = get();
    const node = doc.nodes[id];
    if (node?.kind !== "clock") return;
    const level = to(node.state);
    if (level === node.state) return;
    const nextDoc = {
      ...doc,
      nodes: { ...doc.nodes, [id]: { ...node, state: level } },
    };
    const next = simulate(nextDoc, flopState);
    set({
      doc: nextDoc,
      index: next.index,
      diagnostics: next.diagnostics,
      routes: next.routes,
      flopState: next.flopState,
    });
  };

  const pulseClockSync = (id: NodeId): void => {
    const { doc } = get();
    const node = doc.nodes[id];
    if (node?.kind !== "clock") return;
    if (pulseTimer) {
      clearTimeout(pulseTimer);
      pulseTimer = null;
    }
    if (node.state === 1) {
      setClock(id, () => 0);
    }
    setClock(id, () => 1);
    setClock(id, () => 0);
  };

  const commit = (doc: CircuitDocument, keepOrigin = false): void => {
    const { doc: prev, past, origin, flopState } = get();
    // The stored state is carried through every edit. Adding a wire does not
    // make a counter forget what it was counting, and it is keyed by chip and
    // slot so a rebuilt netlist hands each bit back to the flip-flop it came
    // from rather than to whichever one now has that index.
    const next = simulate(doc, flopState);
    set({
      doc,
      past: [...past, prev].slice(-100),
      future: [],
      index: next.index,
      diagnostics: next.diagnostics,
      routes: next.routes,
      flopState: next.flopState,
      origin: keepOrigin ? origin : null,
    });
  };

  return {
    doc: emptyDocument(),
    flopState: {},
    selection: [],
    pendingPin: null,
    pendingHole: null,
    index: null,
    diagnostics: [],
    routes: new Map(),
    schematic: null,
    origin: null,
    gateInputs: 2,
    activeWireColor: null,
    past: [],
    future: [],

    setGateInputs: (n) => set({ gateInputs: Math.min(4, Math.max(2, n)) }),

    addNode: (partial) => {
      const { doc } = get();
      const id = asNodeId(nextId("n"));
      const label = partial.label ?? defaultLabel(doc, partial);
      const node = { ...partial, id, label } as CircuitNode;

      // On a board, a part has to be PLUGGED IN somewhere. Its schematic
      // coordinates mean nothing here — left as they were, the renderer drew
      // the chip at column 420 of a 63-column board and the camera zoomed out
      // to the whole board trying to reach it.
      const placed = doc.board ? seatOnBoard(doc, node, doc.board) : node;
      if (!placed) return null;

      commit({ ...doc, nodes: { ...doc.nodes, [id]: placed } });
      return id;
    },

    // Called on mouseup, not on every mousemove — the drag itself is tracked in a
    // ref and written straight to the SVG transform. Writing x/y to the store at
    // 60fps would re-render the canvas 60 times a second.
    moveNode: (id, pos) => {
      const { doc } = get();
      const node = doc.nodes[id];
      if (!node) return;

      if (doc.board && node.kind === "ic") {
        const oldCol = Math.round(node.pos.x);
        const newCol = Math.round(pos.x);
        const delta = newCol - oldCol;

        if (delta !== 0) {
          const def = getIc(node.part);
          const pinTotal = def ? def.pins.length : 14;
          const span = pinTotal / 2;
          const terminalRows = new Set(["A", "B", "C", "D", "E", "F", "G", "H", "I", "J"]);

          const shiftEndpoint = (end: (typeof doc.wires)[string]["a"]) => {
            if (
              end.kind === "hole" &&
              terminalRows.has(end.ref.row) &&
              end.ref.col >= oldCol &&
              end.ref.col < oldCol + span
            ) {
              return {
                ...end,
                ref: {
                  ...end.ref,
                  col: end.ref.col + delta,
                },
              };
            }
            return end;
          };

          const nextWires: Record<string, Wire> = {};
          for (const [wId, wire] of Object.entries(doc.wires)) {
            nextWires[wId] = {
              ...wire,
              a: shiftEndpoint(wire.a),
              b: shiftEndpoint(wire.b),
            };
          }

          commit(
            {
              ...doc,
              nodes: { ...doc.nodes, [id]: { ...node, pos } },
              wires: nextWires,
            },
            true,
          );
          return;
        }
      }

      // A move is cosmetic. The circuit is still the one that was built.
      commit({ ...doc, nodes: { ...doc.nodes, [id]: { ...node, pos } } }, true);
    },

    setGateArity: (id, arity) => {
      const { doc } = get();
      const node = doc.nodes[id];
      if (node?.kind !== "gate") return;
      const next = arityOf(node.op, arity);
      if (next === node.arity) return;
      // Widening a gate is a real topology change — the new input pins start
      // unconnected, so this is NOT a keep-origin edit. Any wire to a pin that no
      // longer exists is dropped when the net index rebuilds.
      const gone = new Set<string>();
      if (next < node.arity) {
        for (let i = next; i < node.arity; i++) gone.add(gateInputNames(node.arity)[i]!);
      }
      const wires = Object.fromEntries(
        Object.entries(doc.wires).filter(([, wire]) => {
          const touches = (e: (typeof wire)["a"]) =>
            e.kind === "pin" && e.ref.node === id && gone.has(e.ref.pin);
          return !touches(wire.a) && !touches(wire.b);
        }),
      );
      commit({ ...doc, wires, nodes: { ...doc.nodes, [id]: { ...node, arity: next } } });
    },

    deleteSelected: () => {
      const { doc, selection } = get();
      if (selection.length === 0) return;

      const gone = new Set<string>(selection);
      const nodes = Object.fromEntries(
        Object.entries(doc.nodes).filter(([id]) => !gone.has(id)),
      );
      // Wires to a deleted node go with it. The nets are rebuilt from scratch
      // afterwards, so there is nothing else to clean up.
      const touches = (e: (typeof doc.wires)[string]["a"]): boolean =>
        e.kind === "pin" && gone.has(e.ref.node);
      const wires = Object.fromEntries(
        Object.entries(doc.wires).filter(([, w]) => !touches(w.a) && !touches(w.b)),
      );
      set({ selection: [] });
      commit({ nodes, wires });
    },

    select: (ids) => set({ selection: ids }),

    toggleSwitch: (id) => {
      const { doc } = get();
      const node = doc.nodes[id];
      if (node?.kind !== "switch") return;
      // Flipping a switch is USING the circuit, not editing it.
      commit(
        {
          ...doc,
          nodes: {
            ...doc.nodes,
            [id]: { ...node, state: node.state === 1 ? 0 : 1 },
          },
        },
        true,
      );
    },

    setClock,
    toggleClock: (id) => setClock(id, (level) => (level === 1 ? 0 : 1)),

    /**
     * One complete pulse, as two separate evaluations.
     *
     * In browser, keeps the high state for ~120ms so the user visibly sees the
     * clock LED indicator and wire glow before returning to low.
     * For synchronous execution (headless/tests), pass sync=true or call pulseClockSync.
     */
    pulseClock: (id, sync = false) => {
      if (sync || typeof window === "undefined") {
        pulseClockSync(id);
        return;
      }
      const { doc } = get();
      const node = doc.nodes[id];
      if (node?.kind !== "clock") return;

      if (pulseTimer) {
        clearTimeout(pulseTimer);
        pulseTimer = null;
      }
      // If clock is already high, pull low first to ensure a crisp rising edge
      if (node.state === 1) {
        setClock(id, () => 0);
      }
      setClock(id, () => 1);
      pulseTimer = setTimeout(() => {
        pulseTimer = null;
        setClock(id, () => 0);
      }, 120);
    },

    pulseClockSync,

    /**
     * Power-cycle the memory.
     *
     * Not a document edit — nothing about the circuit changes — so it does not
     * touch the undo stack. It puts every flip-flop back to unknown, which is
     * where a real one starts.
     */
    resetState: () => {
      const { doc } = get();
      const next = simulate(doc, {});
      set({
        flopState: next.flopState,
        index: next.index,
        diagnostics: next.diagnostics,
        routes: next.routes,
      });
    },

    clickPin: (ref) => {
      const { pendingPin, pendingHole, doc } = get();

      if (pendingHole) {
        const id = asWireId(nextId("w"));
        let wireColor = get().activeWireColor ?? undefined;
        const targetNode = doc.nodes[ref.node];
        if (!wireColor && targetNode) {
          if (targetNode.kind === "clock") wireColor = "#f59e0b";
          else if (targetNode.kind === "rail" && targetNode.rail === "vcc") wireColor = "#ef4444";
          else if (targetNode.kind === "rail" && targetNode.rail === "gnd") wireColor = "#3b82f6";
        }
        set({ pendingHole: null, pendingPin: null });
        commit({
          ...doc,
          wires: {
            ...doc.wires,
            [id]: { id, a: holeEnd(pendingHole), b: pinEnd(ref), ...(wireColor ? { color: wireColor } : {}) },
          },
        });
        return;
      }

      if (!pendingPin) {
        set({ pendingPin: ref });
        return;
      }

      // Clicking the same pin twice cancels.
      if (pinKey(pendingPin) === pinKey(ref)) {
        set({ pendingPin: null });
        return;
      }

      const id = asWireId(nextId("w"));
      const wireColor = get().activeWireColor ?? undefined;
      set({ pendingPin: null });
      commit({
        ...doc,
        wires: {
          ...doc.wires,
          [id]: { id, a: pinEnd(pendingPin), b: pinEnd(ref), ...(wireColor ? { color: wireColor } : {}) },
        },
      });
    },

    clickHole: (hole) => {
      const { pendingPin, pendingHole, doc } = get();

      if (pendingPin) {
        const id = asWireId(nextId("w"));
        let color: string | undefined = get().activeWireColor ?? undefined;
        const sourceNode = doc.nodes[pendingPin.node];
        if (!color && sourceNode) {
          if (sourceNode.kind === "clock") color = "#f59e0b";
          else if (sourceNode.kind === "rail" && sourceNode.rail === "vcc") color = "#ef4444";
          else if (sourceNode.kind === "rail" && sourceNode.rail === "gnd") color = "#3b82f6";
        } else if (isPositiveRail(hole.row)) {
          color = "#ef4444";
        } else if (hole.row === "-top" || hole.row === "-bottom") {
          color = "#3b82f6";
        }
        set({ pendingPin: null, pendingHole: null });
        commit({
          ...doc,
          wires: {
            ...doc.wires,
            [id]: { id, a: pinEnd(pendingPin), b: holeEnd(hole), ...(color ? { color } : {}) },
          },
        });
        return;
      }

      if (!pendingHole) {
        set({ pendingHole: hole });
        return;
      }
      // Clicking the same hole twice cancels.
      if (holeKey(pendingHole) === holeKey(hole)) {
        set({ pendingHole: null });
        return;
      }
      // A jumper between two holes on the SAME strip does nothing — the metal
      // already connects them. Say so rather than draw a wire that means nothing.
      if (doc.board && stripOf(doc.board, pendingHole) === stripOf(doc.board, hole)) {
        set({ pendingHole: null });
        return;
      }

      let color: string | undefined = get().activeWireColor ?? undefined;
      if (isPositiveRail(pendingHole.row) || isPositiveRail(hole.row)) {
        color = "#ef4444";
      } else if (
        pendingHole.row === "-top" ||
        pendingHole.row === "-bottom" ||
        hole.row === "-top" ||
        hole.row === "-bottom"
      ) {
        color = "#3b82f6";
      }

      const id = asWireId(nextId("w"));
      set({ pendingHole: null });
      commit({
        ...doc,
        wires: {
          ...doc.wires,
          [id]: { id, a: holeEnd(pendingHole), b: holeEnd(hole), ...(color ? { color } : {}) },
        },
      });
    },

    toBreadboard: (spec) => {
      const { doc } = get();
      if (doc.board) return [];

      const { doc: placed, unplaced } = spec
        ? placeOnBreadboard(doc, spec, { panelMode: true })
        : placeOnBreadboard(doc, DEFAULT_BOARD, { panelMode: true });
      const { index, diagnostics, routes, flopState } = simulate(placed, get().flopState);
      set({
        doc: placed,
        flopState,
        // Keep the schematic. Seating is a realization, not a translation — the
        // board has no memory of the layout it came from, so the only honest way
        // back is to have kept it.
        schematic: doc,
        index,
        diagnostics,
        routes,
        past: [],
        future: [],
        selection: [],
        pendingPin: null,
        pendingHole: null,
      });
      return unplaced;
    },

    toSchematic: () => {
      const { schematic, doc } = get();
      if (!doc.board) return true; // already there
      if (!schematic) return false; // built on the board; there is nothing to go back to

      const { index, diagnostics, routes, flopState } = simulate(schematic, get().flopState);
      set({
        doc: schematic,
        flopState,
        index,
        diagnostics,
        routes,
        past: [],
        future: [],
        selection: [],
        pendingPin: null,
        pendingHole: null,
      });
      return true;
    },

    cancelWire: () => set({ pendingPin: null, pendingHole: null }),

    deleteWire: (id) => {
      const { doc } = get();
      const wires = { ...doc.wires };
      delete wires[id];
      commit({ ...doc, wires });
    },

    setWireColor: (id, color) => {
      const { doc } = get();
      const wire = doc.wires[id];
      if (!wire) return;
      commit({
        ...doc,
        wires: {
          ...doc.wires,
          [id]: { ...wire, color },
        },
      });
    },

    setActiveWireColor: (color) => set({ activeWireColor: color }),

    setLedColor: (id, color) => {
      const { doc } = get();
      const node = doc.nodes[id];
      if (!node || node.kind !== "led") return;
      commit(
        {
          ...doc,
          nodes: {
            ...doc.nodes,
            [id]: { ...node, color },
          },
        },
        true,
      );
    },

    rotateNode: (id, delta = 90) => {
      const { doc } = get();
      const node = doc.nodes[id];
      if (!node) return;
      const current = node.rotation ?? 0;
      const next = ((((current + delta) % 360) + 360) % 360) as 0 | 90 | 180 | 270;
      commit({
        ...doc,
        nodes: {
          ...doc.nodes,
          [id]: { ...node, rotation: next },
        },
      });
    },

    flipNode: (id, axis) => {
      const { doc } = get();
      const node = doc.nodes[id];
      if (!node) return;
      const updated =
        axis === "x"
          ? { ...node, flipX: !node.flipX }
          : { ...node, flipY: !node.flipY };
      commit({
        ...doc,
        nodes: {
          ...doc.nodes,
          [id]: updated,
        },
      });
    },

    autoWireIcPower: (nodeId) => {
      const { doc } = get();
      const node = doc.nodes[nodeId];
      if (!node || node.kind !== "ic" || !doc.board) return;
      const def = getIc(node.part);
      if (!def) return;
      const col = Math.round(node.pos.x);
      const holes = dipHoles({ col }, def.pins.length);
      const vccIdx = def.pins.findIndex((p) => p.dir === "pwr");
      const gndIdx = def.pins.findIndex((p) => p.dir === "gnd");
      if (vccIdx === -1 || gndIdx === -1) return;

      const vccLeg = holes[vccIdx]!;
      const gndLeg = holes[gndIdx]!;

      // Upper half (row F): free hole is row J -> +top rail; lower half (row E): row A -> -bottom rail
      const vccHole: HoleRef = { col: vccLeg.col, row: vccLeg.row === "F" ? "J" : "A" };
      const vccRail: HoleRef = { col: vccLeg.col, row: vccLeg.row === "F" ? "+top" : "+bottom" };
      const gndHole: HoleRef = { col: gndLeg.col, row: gndLeg.row === "E" ? "A" : "J" };
      const gndRail: HoleRef = { col: gndLeg.col, row: gndLeg.row === "E" ? "-bottom" : "-top" };

      const w1 = asWireId(nextId("w"));
      const w2 = asWireId(nextId("w"));

      commit({
        ...doc,
        wires: {
          ...doc.wires,
          [w1]: { id: w1, a: holeEnd(vccHole), b: holeEnd(vccRail), color: "#ef4444" },
          [w2]: { id: w2, a: holeEnd(gndHole), b: holeEnd(gndRail), color: "#3b82f6" },
        },
      });
    },

    load: (doc, origin = null) => {
      // A freshly loaded circuit has not been clocked. Carrying the old state in
      // would have its flip-flops start at values from a different circuit.
      const { index, diagnostics, routes, flopState } = simulate(doc, {});
      set({
        doc,
        origin,
        flopState,
        index,
        diagnostics,
        routes,
        // A freshly loaded board did not come from the old one, so the old schematic
        // is not a place it can go "back" to.
        schematic: null,
        past: [],
        future: [],
        selection: [],
        pendingPin: null,
        pendingHole: null,
      });
    },

    clear: () => {
      const currentDoc = get().doc;
      const nextDoc = emptyDocument();
      const { index, diagnostics, routes, flopState } = simulate(nextDoc, {});
      set({
        doc: nextDoc,
        origin: null,
        flopState,
        schematic: null,
        index,
        diagnostics,
        routes,
        past: [...get().past, currentDoc],
        future: [],
        selection: [],
        pendingPin: null,
        pendingHole: null,
      });
    },

    undo: () => {
      const { past, doc, future } = get();
      const prev = past[past.length - 1];
      if (!prev) return;
      const { index, diagnostics, routes, flopState } = simulate(prev, get().flopState);
      set({
        doc: prev,
        flopState,
        past: past.slice(0, -1),
        future: [doc, ...future],
        index,
        diagnostics,
        routes,
        selection: [],
        pendingPin: null,
      });
    },

    redo: () => {
      const { future, doc, past } = get();
      const next = future[0];
      if (!next) return;
      const { index, diagnostics, routes, flopState } = simulate(next, get().flopState);
      set({
        doc: next,
        flopState,
        past: [...past, doc],
        future: future.slice(1),
        index,
        diagnostics,
        routes,
        selection: [],
        pendingPin: null,
      });
    },
      };
    },
    {
      name: "digilab-circuit",

      /**
       * ONLY THE DOCUMENT IS PERSISTED. Nets, routes, diagnostics and simulation
       * values are all DERIVED (Invariants 1 and 6) — storing them would let a
       * stale snapshot outlive the topology it came from, which is precisely the
       * ghost-connection failure the invariants exist to prevent. Undo history is
       * dropped too: nobody expects to reopen a tab and undo yesterday.
       */
      partialize: (s) => ({ doc: s.doc, schematic: s.schematic, origin: s.origin }),

      /**
       * Rehydration must RE-DERIVE, not restore. The document is plain JSON, so it
       * survives the round trip intact; everything else is rebuilt from it exactly
       * as it would be after any other topology change.
       */
      onRehydrateStorage: () => (state) => {
        if (!state) return;
        const { index, diagnostics, routes, flopState } = simulate(state.doc, state.flopState);
        useCircuitStore.setState({ index, diagnostics, routes, flopState });
      },
    },
  ),
);

/** Convenience for the palette. */
export const gateNode = (op: GateOp, pos: Point, arity = 2) => ({
  kind: "gate" as const,
  op,
  arity: arityOf(op, arity),
  pos,
});


=== FILE: src/components/lab/clock-panel.tsx ===
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Pause, Play, RotateCcw, SkipForward, Zap } from "lucide-react";

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Card } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Tooltip, TooltipContent, TooltipTrigger } from "@/components/ui/tooltip";
import { useCircuitStore } from "@/stores/circuit-store";
import { LOGIC_NAMES, type Logic } from "@/lib/simulation/logic";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";

/**
 * The controls a clocked circuit needs, and only appears when there is one.
 *
 * A combinational circuit is driven entirely by its switches: set them, read the
 * answer. A sequential one cannot be driven that way at all — the whole point is
 * that its output depends on a sequence of EDGES, and there is no way to produce
 * an edge by setting a level. So the clock gets its own controls, and the lab is
 * unusable for counters without them.
 *
 * PULSE, NOT TOGGLE, IS THE PRIMARY ACTION. Half the parts here load on the
 * rising edge ('74, '175) and half on the falling one ('76, '107), so a button
 * that only raised the clock would appear to do nothing at all on a ripple
 * counter. One press runs the level up and back down, which advances either.
 */
export function ClockPanel() {
  const doc = useCircuitStore((s) => s.doc);
  const pulseClock = useCircuitStore((s) => s.pulseClock);
  const toggleClock = useCircuitStore((s) => s.toggleClock);
  const resetState = useCircuitStore((s) => s.resetState);
  const flopState = useCircuitStore((s) => s.flopState);

  const clocks = Object.values(doc.nodes).filter((n) => n.kind === "clock");
  const stored = Object.entries(flopState);

  const [running, setRunning] = useState(false);
  const [hz, setHz] = useState(2);

  /**
   * Free-running.
   *
   * The tick reads the store rather than closing over the clocks it found at
   * render time, which is both simpler and more correct: it pulses whatever
   * clocks exist NOW, so adding or deleting one mid-run does the obvious thing
   * instead of driving a stale list.
   */
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!running) return;
    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      const state = useCircuitStore.getState();
      for (const node of Object.values(state.doc.nodes)) {
        if (node.kind === "clock") state.toggleClock(node.id);
      }
    }, 1000 / (2 * hz));
    return () => {
      clearInterval(timer);
      const state = useCircuitStore.getState();
      for (const node of Object.values(state.doc.nodes)) {
        if (node.kind === "clock" && node.state === 1) {
          state.setClock(node.id, () => 0);
        }
      }
    };
  }, [running, hz]);

  // Nothing clocked on the board: say nothing. A panel of controls for a thing
  // that is not there is worse than no panel.
  if (clocks.length === 0 && stored.length === 0) return null;

  return (
    <Card className="flex shrink-0 flex-row flex-wrap items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2">
      <span className="text-muted-foreground flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium">
        <Zap className="size-3.5" />
        Clock
      </span>

      {clocks.length === 0 ? (
        <span className="text-muted-foreground text-xs text-pretty">
          This circuit has flip-flops but no clock source. Add one from the palette
          and wire it to CLK.
        </span>
      ) : (
        <>
          {clocks.map((clock) => (
            <div key={clock.id} className="flex items-center gap-1">
              <span className="font-mono text-[11px]">{clock.label}</span>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => toggleClock(clock.id)}
                title="Toggle this clock — one edge"
                className={cn(
                  "rounded border px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-[10px]",
                  clock.kind === "clock" && clock.state === 1
                    ? "border-logic-high text-logic-high"
                    : "text-muted-foreground",
                )}
              >
                {clock.kind === "clock" && clock.state === 1 ? "HIGH" : "LOW"}
              </button>
            </div>
          ))}

          <Tooltip>
            <TooltipTrigger asChild>
              <Button
                size="sm"
                variant="secondary"
                className="h-7 gap-1.5 px-2 text-xs"
                onClick={() => clocks.forEach((c) => pulseClock(c.id))}
              >
                <SkipForward className="size-3.5" />
                Pulse
              </Button>
            </TooltipTrigger>
            <TooltipContent className="max-w-64">
              One complete pulse — up and back down — so a rising-edge part and a
              falling-edge part both advance.
            </TooltipContent>
          </Tooltip>

          <Button
            size="sm"
            variant={running ? "destructive" : "outline"}
            className="h-7 gap-1.5 px-2 text-xs"
            onClick={() => setRunning((r) => !r)}
          >
            {running ? <Pause className="size-3.5" /> : <Play className="size-3.5" />}
            {running ? "Stop" : "Run"}
          </Button>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <input
              type="range"
              min={1}
              max={10}
              value={hz}
              onChange={(e) => setHz(Number(e.target.value))}
              className="accent-primary h-1.5 w-20 cursor-pointer"
              aria-label="Clock rate"
            />
            <span className="text-muted-foreground w-10 font-mono text-[10px] tabular-nums">
              {hz} Hz
            </span>
          </div>
        </>
      )}

      {stored.length > 0 && (
        <>
          <span className="text-muted-foreground ml-auto flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px]">
            <span className="font-medium">State</span>
            <span className="font-mono">
              {stored.map(([key, [q]]) => (
                <span key={key} className="ml-1" title={key}>
                  {LOGIC_NAMES[q as Logic]}
                </span>
              ))}
            </span>
          </span>
          <Tooltip>
            <TooltipTrigger asChild>
              <Button
                size="sm"
                variant="ghost"
                className="h-7 gap-1.5 px-2 text-xs"
                onClick={resetState}
              >
                <RotateCcw className="size-3.5" />
                Power-cycle
              </Button>
            </TooltipTrigger>
            <TooltipContent className="max-w-64">
              Put every flip-flop back to unknown, where a real one starts. Not an
              edit — it does not touch undo.
            </TooltipContent>
          </Tooltip>
        </>
      )}
    </Card>
  );
}


=== FILE: src/components/lab/waveform-panel.tsx ===
"use client";

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Zap } from "lucide-react";
import { Alert, AlertDescription, AlertTitle } from "@/components/ui/alert";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { useCircuitStore } from "@/stores/circuit-store";
import { elaborate } from "@/lib/simulation/elaborate";
import { MAX_SWEEP_INPUTS, simulateTimed, type Waveform } from "@/lib/simulation/timing";
import { toSeqState } from "@/lib/simulation/solver";
import { L1, LX, LZ, type Logic } from "@/lib/simulation/logic";

const ROW_H = 34;
const WAVE_H = 20;
const LABEL_W = 44;
const TICK_W = 7;

/**
 * The timing panel.
 *
 * Two things make this worth having rather than decorative. The inputs are driven
 * by a GRAY-CODE counter, so the diagram sweeps the whole truth table one bit at
 * a time and becomes a logic-analyzer view of the function. And the solver models
 * unit gate delay, so STATIC HAZARDS show up as real glitches — which the
 * settle-to-fixpoint solver on the canvas cannot show, because it converges past
 * them.
 */
export function WaveformPanel() {
  const doc = useCircuitStore((s) => s.doc);
  const [delay, setDelay] = useState(1);

  // The clocked chart continues from where the board actually is, so a counter
  // you have just cleared charts a count and one you have not charts unknowns —
  // which is the truth about an uncleared counter, and the lesson.
  const flopState = useCircuitStore((s) => s.flopState);
  const result = useMemo(() => {
    const netlist = elaborate(doc).netlist;
    return simulateTimed(netlist, { delay, state: toSeqState(netlist, flopState) });
  }, [doc, delay, flopState]);

  // Too many inputs to sweep. Say which circuit and by how much, rather than
  // fall through to the "add some switches" line — the user has just built
  // something substantial and being told to add a switch reads as a bug.
  if (result.tooWide) {
    return (
      <p className="text-muted-foreground p-3 text-sm text-pretty">
        {result.tooWide.inputs} inputs is{" "}
        {result.tooWide.combinations.toLocaleString()} combinations — too many to
        sweep, and far too many to read. The timing diagram covers circuits with up
        to {MAX_SWEEP_INPUTS} inputs; the schematic, the faults and the verification
        all still work here.
      </p>
    );
  }

  if (result.ticks === 0) {
    return (
      <p className="text-muted-foreground p-3 text-sm">
        Add input switches, some gates, and an LED, and the timing diagram will
        sweep every input combination.
      </p>
    );
  }

  const width = LABEL_W + result.ticks * TICK_W;
  const height = result.waves.length * ROW_H + 18;
  const glitchTicks = new Set(result.glitches.flatMap((g) => g.ticks));

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3 p-3">
      {result.glitches.length > 0 && (
        <Alert>
          <Zap className="text-logic-z" />
          <AlertTitle>Glitch detected</AlertTitle>
          <AlertDescription>
            {result.glitches.map((g) => g.label).join(", ")} pulses away from its
            settled value while the inputs are held steady — a{" "}
            <strong>static hazard</strong>, caused by unequal path delays rather
            than by a logic error. The truth table is still correct, but on real
            hardware that spike is a real pulse. The cure is a redundant consensus
            term: the one Quine–McCluskey discarded <em>because</em> it was
            redundant.
          </AlertDescription>
        </Alert>
      )}

      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <span className="text-muted-foreground text-xs">Gate delay</span>
        {[1, 2, 3].map((d) => (
          <Button
            key={d}
            size="sm"
            variant={delay === d ? "secondary" : "ghost"}
            className="h-7 px-2 font-mono text-xs"
            onClick={() => setDelay(d)}
          >
            {d}
          </Button>
        ))}
        <span className="text-muted-foreground ml-auto text-xs">
          {result.clocked
            ? `${result.clocked.cycles} clock cycles`
            : "Gray-code sweep, forward then back"}
        </span>
      </div>

      <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-md border">
        <svg width={width} height={height} className="block">
          {/* Step boundaries. */}
          {Array.from({ length: result.steps + 1 }, (_, s) => {
            const x = LABEL_W + (result.settleTicks + s * result.ticksPerCombination) * TICK_W;
            return (
              <line
                key={s}
                x1={x}
                y1={4}
                x2={x}
                y2={height - 8}
                className="stroke-border"
                strokeDasharray="2 3"
              />
            );
          })}

          {/* Glitch highlights, behind the traces. */}
          {[...glitchTicks].map((t) => (
            <rect
              key={t}
              x={LABEL_W + t * TICK_W}
              y={4}
              width={TICK_W}
              height={height - 12}
              style={{ fill: "var(--logic-z)" }}
              opacity={0.18}
            />
          ))}

          {result.waves.map((wave, row) => (
            <WaveRow key={wave.label} wave={wave} row={row} />
          ))}
        </svg>
      </div>

      <p className="text-muted-foreground text-xs">
        Inputs step through every combination changing one bit at a time, then
        reverse — so each transition is exercised in both directions. A hazard is
        directional: it appears on a falling edge but not the matching rising one.
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}

function WaveRow({ wave, row }: { wave: Waveform; row: number }) {
  const top = 8 + row * ROW_H;
  const mid = top + WAVE_H / 2;

  // Digital waveform: horizontal runs joined by vertical edges.
  const yOf = (v: Logic): number =>
    v === L1 ? top : v === LZ || v === LX ? mid : top + WAVE_H;

  let d = "";
  let prev: Logic | null = null;
  wave.samples.forEach((v, t) => {
    const x = LABEL_W + t * TICK_W;
    const y = yOf(v);
    if (prev === null) d += `M ${x} ${y}`;
    else if (v !== prev) d += ` L ${x} ${yOf(prev)} L ${x} ${y}`;
    d += ` L ${x + TICK_W} ${y}`;
    prev = v;
  });

  const undefinedRuns = wave.samples
    .map((v, t) => (v === LX || v === LZ ? t : -1))
    .filter((t) => t >= 0);

  return (
    <g>
      <text
        x={LABEL_W - 8}
        y={mid + 4}
        textAnchor="end"
        className="fill-foreground font-mono text-[11px] font-medium"
      >
        {wave.label}
      </text>

      {/* Z / X stretches sit on the midline and are drawn in their own colour, so
          "floating" never looks like "low". */}
      {undefinedRuns.map((t) => (
        <rect
          key={t}
          x={LABEL_W + t * TICK_W}
          y={mid - 1.5}
          width={TICK_W}
          height={3}
          style={{ fill: wave.samples[t] === LZ ? "var(--logic-z)" : "var(--logic-x)" }}
        />
      ))}

      <path
        d={d}
        fill="none"
        strokeWidth={1.75}
        style={{ stroke: wave.isInput ? "var(--logic-low)" : "var(--logic-high)" }}
        strokeLinejoin="round"
      />
    </g>
  );
}

/**

 * - Authentic 5-hole terminal strip connectivity & live 4-state probing
 * - 74xx DIP-14 internal gate X-Ray overlays & Pin-1 orientation ergonomics
 * - Horizontal IC dragging along the center ravine with collision prevention
 * - Safe wire selection, auto-coloring, and deletion UX
 */
export function BreadboardView() {
  const doc = useCircuitStore((s) => s.doc);
  const spec = doc.board ?? DEFAULT_BOARD;

  const clickHole = useCircuitStore((s) => s.clickHole);
  const pendingHole = useCircuitStore((s) => s.pendingHole);
  const clickPin = useCircuitStore((s) => s.clickPin);
  const pendingPin = useCircuitStore((s) => s.pendingPin);
  const cancelWire = useCircuitStore((s) => s.cancelWire);
  const deleteWire = useCircuitStore((s) => s.deleteWire);
  const setWireColor = useCircuitStore((s) => s.setWireColor);
  const setLedColor = useCircuitStore((s) => s.setLedColor);
  const autoWireIcPower = useCircuitStore((s) => s.autoWireIcPower);
  const toggleSwitch = useCircuitStore((s) => s.toggleSwitch);
  const toggleClock = useCircuitStore((s) => s.toggleClock);
  const pulseClock = useCircuitStore((s) => s.pulseClock);
  const diagnostics = useCircuitStore((s) => s.diagnostics);
  const selection = useCircuitStore((s) => s.selection);
  const select = useCircuitStore((s) => s.select);
  const deleteSelected = useCircuitStore((s) => s.deleteSelected);
  const moveNode = useCircuitStore((s) => s.moveNode);
  const addNode = useCircuitStore((s) => s.addNode);

  const constraint = useConstraint();
  const setSpec = useSpecStore((s) => s.setSpec);
  const clearSpec = useSpecStore((s) => s.clear);
  const load = useCircuitStore((s) => s.load);
  const toBreadboard = useCircuitStore((s) => s.toBreadboard);
  const toSchematic = useCircuitStore((s) => s.toSchematic);
  const clearCircuit = useCircuitStore((s) => s.clear);

  const [hover, setHover] = useState<HoleRef | null>(null);
  const [hoverSocket, setHoverSocket] = useState<{ point: Point; pin: PinRef; label: string; color: string } | null>(null);
  const [cursorPos, setCursorPos] = useState<Point | null>(null);
  const hoverValue = useEndpointValue(hover ? holeEnd(hover) : null);
  const [selectedWireId, setSelectedWireId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const selectedWire = selectedWireId ? doc.wires[selectedWireId as WireId] : null;
  const [showXRay, setShowXRay] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [showAirwires, setShowAirwires] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [autoHz, setAutoHz] = useState<number | null>(null);

  const nodes = useMemo(() => Object.values(doc.nodes), [doc.nodes]);

  const vccNode = useMemo(
    () => nodes.find((n) => n.kind === "rail" && n.rail === "vcc") ?? null,
    [nodes],
  );
  const gndNode = useMemo(
    () => nodes.find((n) => n.kind === "rail" && n.rail === "gnd") ?? null,
    [nodes],
  );
  const clockNode = useMemo(
    () => nodes.find((n): n is Extract<CircuitNode, { kind: "clock" }> => n.kind === "clock") ?? null,
    [nodes],
  );
  const switchNodes = useMemo(
    () => nodes.filter((n): n is Extract<CircuitNode, { kind: "switch" }> => n.kind === "switch"),
    [nodes],
  );
  const ledNodes = useMemo(
    () => nodes.filter((n): n is Extract<CircuitNode, { kind: "led" }> => n.kind === "led"),

  );
  const icNodes = useMemo(
    () => nodes.filter((n): n is Extract<CircuitNode, { kind: "ic" }> => n.kind === "ic"),
    [nodes],
  );

  const unpoweredNodes = useMemo(
    () => icNodes.filter((n) => diagnostics.some((d) => d.code === "UNPOWERED_IC" && d.node === n.id)),
    [icNodes, diagnostics],
  );

  // Auto-clock effect: periodic 50% duty cycle square wave at selected frequency
  useEffect(() => {
    if (autoHz === null) return;
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      const state = useCircuitStore.getState();
      let clk = Object.values(state.doc.nodes).find((n): n is ClockNode => n.kind === "clock");
      if (!clk) {
        const id = state.addNode({ kind: "clock", label: "CLK", state: 0, pos: { x: 0, y: 0 } });
        if (id) {
          const n = state.doc.nodes[id];
          if (n?.kind === "clock") clk = n;
        }
      }
      if (clk) {
        state.toggleClock(clk.id);
      }
    }, 1000 / (2 * autoHz));
    return () => {
      clearInterval(interval);
      const state = useCircuitStore.getState();
      const clk = Object.values(state.doc.nodes).find((n) => n.kind === "clock");
      if (clk && clk.state === 1) {
        state.setClock(clk.id, () => 0);
      }
    };
  }, [autoHz]);

  // Exact coordinates of all Trainer Panel sockets
  const trainerPinMap = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, Point>();
    const register = (pin: PinRef, pt: Point) => {
      map.set(pinKey(pin), pt);
      map.set(`${pin.node}:${pin.pin}`, pt);
    };

    const vccId = vccNode ? vccNode.id : asNodeId("pending_vcc");
    const gndId = gndNode ? gndNode.id : asNodeId("pending_gnd");
    const clkId = clockNode ? clockNode.id : asNodeId("pending_clk");
    register({ node: vccId, pin: "VCC" }, { x: 430, y: 60 });
    register({ node: gndId, pin: "GND" }, { x: 520, y: 60 });
    register({ node: clkId, pin: "Y" }, { x: 850, y: 55 });

    switchNodes.forEach((sw, i) => {
      register({ node: sw.id, pin: "Y" }, { x: 105 + i * 82 + 18, y: 646 });
    });

    ledNodes.forEach((led, i) => {
      register({ node: led.id, pin: "A" }, { x: 945 + i * 66, y: 72 });
    });

    return map;
  }, [vccNode, gndNode, clockNode, switchNodes, ledNodes]);

  // List of all Trainer Panel sockets for magnetic hovering and snapping
  const trainerSocketsList = useMemo(() => {
    const list: { point: Point; pin: PinRef; label: string; color: string }[] = [];
    const vccId = vccNode ? vccNode.id : asNodeId("pending_vcc");
    const gndId = gndNode ? gndNode.id : asNodeId("pending_gnd");
    const clkId = clockNode ? clockNode.id : asNodeId("pending_clk");

    list.push({ point: { x: 430, y: 60 }, pin: { node: vccId, pin: "VCC" }, label: "+5V", color: "#ef4444" });
    list.push({ point: { x: 520, y: 60 }, pin: { node: gndId, pin: "GND" }, label: "GND", color: "#3b82f6" });
    list.push({ point: { x: 850, y: 55 }, pin: { node: clkId, pin: "Y" }, label: "CLK", color: "#f59e0b" });
    switchNodes.forEach((sw, i) => {
      list.push({ point: { x: 105 + i * 82 + 18, y: 646 }, pin: { node: sw.id, pin: "Y" }, label: sw.label, color: "#38bdf8" });
    });
    ledNodes.forEach((led, i) => {
      list.push({ point: { x: 945 + i * 66, y: 72 }, pin: { node: led.id, pin: "A" }, label: led.label, color: led.color ?? "#22c55e" });
    });
    return list;
  }, [vccNode, gndNode, clockNode, switchNodes, ledNodes]);

  const pendingStart = useMemo<Point | null>(() => {
    if (pendingHole) {
      const p = holePoint(pendingHole);
      return { x: BOARD_OFFSET_X + p.x, y: BOARD_OFFSET_Y + p.y };
    }
    if (pendingPin) {
      return (
        trainerPinMap.get(pinKey(pendingPin)) ??
        trainerPinMap.get(`${pendingPin.node}:${pendingPin.pin}`) ??
        null
      );
    }
    return null;
  }, [pendingHole, pendingPin, trainerPinMap]);

  const pendingTarget = useMemo<Point | null>(() => {
    if (!pendingStart) return null;
    if (hoverSocket) return hoverSocket.point;
    if (hover) {
      const p = holePoint(hover);
      return { x: BOARD_OFFSET_X + p.x, y: BOARD_OFFSET_Y + p.y };
    }
    return cursorPos;
  }, [pendingStart, hoverSocket, hover, cursorPos]);

  // Dedicated Manhattan routing channels
  const channelConfig = useMemo<BreadboardChannelConfig>(
    () => ({
      topPanelGutterY: 105,
      upperStripGutterY: 190,
      centerRavineY: 346,
      lowerStripGutterY: 502,
      bottomPanelGutterY: 572,
      laneSpacing: 5,
      cornerRadius: 6,
    }),
    [],
  );

  // IC Obstacle Bounding Boxes for Obstacle Avoidance
  const icObstacles = useMemo<BoundingBox[]>(() => {
    return icNodes.map((ic) => {
      const def = getIc(ic.part);
      const pinTotal = def ? def.pins.length : 14;
      const span = pinTotal / 2;
      const col = Math.round(ic.pos.x);
      const holes = dipHoles({ col }, pinTotal);
      const first = holes[0];
      const last = holes[span - 1];
      if (!first || !last) return { id: ic.id, minX: 0, minY: 0, maxX: 0, maxY: 0 };
      const a = holePoint(first);
      const b = holePoint(last);
      const top = holePoint({ col: first.col, row: "F" }).y;
      const bottom = a.y;
      return {
        id: ic.id,
        minX: BOARD_OFFSET_X + a.x - PITCH / 2 + 2,
        maxX: BOARD_OFFSET_X + b.x + PITCH / 2 - 2,
        minY: BOARD_OFFSET_Y + top - 3,
        maxY: BOARD_OFFSET_Y + bottom + 3,
      };
    });
  }, [icNodes]);

  // Optional custom horizontal channel offset per wire (from dragging)
  const [wireCustomY, setWireCustomY] = useState<Record<string, number>>({});

  // Prepare WireRouteInput[] for all jumpers
  const wireInputs = useMemo<WireRouteInput[]>(() => {
    const list: WireRouteInput[] = [];
    for (const wire of Object.values(doc.wires)) {
      const a = resolveEndpointPoint(wire.a, trainerPinMap, doc.nodes);
      const b = resolveEndpointPoint(wire.b, trainerPinMap, doc.nodes);
      if (!a || !b) continue;
      list.push({
        id: wire.id,
        from: a,
        to: b,
        color: wire.color || "#38bdf8",
        customChannelY: wireCustomY[wire.id],
      });
    }
    return list;
  }, [doc.wires, trainerPinMap, doc.nodes, wireCustomY]);

  // Compute clean, non-overlapping orthogonal routes
  const routedWires = useMemo(() => {
    return routeBreadboardWires(wireInputs, channelConfig, icObstacles);
  }, [wireInputs, channelConfig, icObstacles]);

  const routedWireMap = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, RoutedWire>();
    for (const rw of routedWires) {
      map.set(rw.id, rw);
    }
    return map;
  }, [routedWires]);

  // Keyboard shortcuts: X (X-Ray), A (Airwires), Delete/Backspace (Delete wire or IC), Escape (Cancel)
  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      const el = document.activeElement;
      if (el instanceof HTMLInputElement || el instanceof HTMLTextAreaElement) return;

      if (e.key === "x" || e.key === "X") {
        setShowXRay((v) => !v);
      } else if (e.key === "a" || e.key === "A") {
        setShowAirwires((v) => !v);
      } else if (e.key === "Delete" || e.key === "Backspace") {
        if (selectedWireId) {
          deleteWire(selectedWireId as WireId);
          setSelectedWireId(null);
          toast.info("Deleted jumper wire");
        } else if (selection.length > 0) {
          deleteSelected();
          toast.info("Deleted component");
        }
      } else if (e.key === "Escape") {
        setSelectedWireId(null);
        select([]);
        cancelWire();
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, [selectedWireId, selection, deleteWire, deleteSelected, select, cancelWire]);

  const handleSynthesizeTheory = () => {
    const src = theorySource?.trim() || "F(A,B,C) = A'B + BC";
    const rebuilt = rebuildUnder(src, constraint);
    if (!rebuilt.ok) {
      toast.error("Could not synthesize expression", { description: rebuilt.reason });
      return;
    }
    setSpec(src);
    load(rebuilt.doc, src);
    toBreadboard();
    toast.success("Synthesized onto breadboard", {
      description: describeDesign(rebuilt.design),
    });
  };

  const handleLoadStarterPreset = () => {
    const preset = PRESETS[0];
    if (!preset) return;
    load(buildPreset(preset));
    clearSpec();
    toBreadboard();
    toast.success(`Loaded ${preset.name}`, {
      description: preset.note ?? "Seated on breadboard",
    });
  };

  const handleSwitchToSchematic = () => {
    if (!toSchematic()) {
      clearCircuit();
    }
  };

  const handleInitTrainerDefaults = () => {
    ["A", "B", "C", "D"].forEach((lbl) => {
      addNode({ kind: "switch", label: lbl, state: 0, pos: { x: 0, y: 0 } });
    });
    ["L0", "L1", "L2", "L3"].forEach((lbl) => {
      addNode({ kind: "led", label: lbl, pos: { x: 0, y: 0 } });
    });
    if (!vccNode) addNode({ kind: "rail", rail: "vcc", label: "+5V", pos: { x: 0, y: 0 } });
    if (!gndNode) addNode({ kind: "rail", rail: "gnd", label: "GND", pos: { x: 0, y: 0 } });
    if (!clockNode) addNode({ kind: "clock", label: "CLK", state: 0, pos: { x: 0, y: 0 } });
    toast.success("Initialized Digital Logic Trainer Bench with 4 Switches, 4 LEDs, and Power");
  };

  const handleAddSwitch = () => {
    const letter = String.fromCharCode(65 + (switchNodes.length % 26));
    addNode({ kind: "switch", label: letter, state: 0, pos: { x: 0, y: 0 } });
    toast.success(`Added Switch ${letter}`);
  };

  const handleAddLed = () => {
    const lbl = `L${ledNodes.length}`;
    addNode({ kind: "led", label: lbl, pos: { x: 0, y: 0 } });
    toast.success(`Added LED ${lbl}`);
  };

  const handleVccClick = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    const vcc = nodes.find((n) => n.kind === "rail" && n.rail === "vcc");
    if (!vcc) {
      const id = addNode({ kind: "rail", rail: "vcc", label: "+5V", pos: { x: 0, y: 0 } });
      if (id) clickPin({ node: id, pin: "VCC" });
    } else {
      clickPin({ node: vcc.id, pin: "VCC" });
    }
  };

  const handleGndClick = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    const gnd = nodes.find((n) => n.kind === "rail" && n.rail === "gnd");
    if (!gnd) {
      const id = addNode({ kind: "rail", rail: "gnd", label: "GND", pos: { x: 0, y: 0 } });
      if (id) clickPin({ node: id, pin: "GND" });
    } else {
      clickPin({ node: gnd.id, pin: "GND" });
    }
  };

  const handleClockClick = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    const clk = nodes.find((n) => n.kind === "clock");
    if (!clk) {
      const id = addNode({ kind: "clock", label: "CLK", state: 0, pos: { x: 0, y: 0 } });
      if (id) clickPin({ node: id, pin: "Y" });
    } else {
      clickPin({ node: clk.id, pin: "Y" });
    }
  };

  const handlePulse = (e: React.MouseEvent) => {
    e.stopPropagation();
    const clk = nodes.find((n) => n.kind === "clock");
    if (!clk) {
      const id = addNode({ kind: "clock", label: "CLK", state: 0, pos: { x: 0, y: 0 } });
      if (id) pulseClock(id);
    } else {
      pulseClock(clk.id);
    }
  };

  const w = boardWidth(spec);
  const h = boardHeight();
  const CHASSIS_W = Math.max(
    BOARD_OFFSET_X * 2 + w,
    945 + (ledNodes.length + 1) * 66 + 40,
    105 + (switchNodes.length + 1) * 82 + 80,
  );
  const CHASSIS_H = BOARD_OFFSET_Y + h + 115;

  /** Fit the camera to the full trainer chassis */
  const natural = useMemo(() => {
    return { x: 0, y: 0, w: Math.max(1200, CHASSIS_W), h: CHASSIS_H };
  }, [CHASSIS_W, CHASSIS_H]);

  const svgRef = useRef<SVGSVGElement>(null);
  const cam = useViewBox(natural, svgRef);
  const dragStartRef = useRef<{ x: number; y: number } | null>(null);

  return (
    <div className="relative h-full w-full">
      {/* Top Breadboard Action Toolbar */}
      <div className="no-print absolute right-2 top-2 z-10 flex items-center gap-1.5">
        {unpoweredNodes.length > 0 && (
          <Button
            variant="outline"
            size="sm"
            className="h-7 text-xs border-amber-500/50 bg-card/90 text-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-500/10 shadow-xs"
            onClick={() => {
              unpoweredNodes.forEach((n) => autoWireIcPower(n.id));
              toast.success(`Auto-powered ${unpoweredNodes.length} IC${unpoweredNodes.length > 1 ? "s" : ""}`);
            }}
            title="Auto-wire VCC and GND to power rails for all unpowered ICs"
          >
            <Zap className="size-3.5 mr-1 fill-amber-500" />
            <span>Auto-Power ({unpoweredNodes.length})</span>
          </Button>
        )}
        <Button
          variant={showXRay ? "default" : "outline"}
          size="sm"
          className="h-7 text-xs gap-1 shadow-xs"
          onClick={() => setShowXRay((v) => !v)}
          title="Toggle DIP-14 Internal Gate X-Ray View (Hotkey X)"
        >
          {showXRay ? <Eye className="size-3.5" /> : <EyeOff className="size-3.5 text-muted-foreground" />}
          <span>X-Ray</span>
          <kbd className="ml-0.5 rounded bg-muted/40 px-1 text-[9px] font-mono">X</kbd>
        </Button>
        <Button
          variant={showAirwires ? "default" : "outline"}
          size="sm"
          className="h-7 text-xs gap-1 shadow-xs"
          onClick={() => setShowAirwires((v) => !v)}
          title="Toggle Unrouted Schematic Airwires Guide (Hotkey A)"
        >
          <Radio className="size-3.5" />
          <span>Airwires</span>
          <kbd className="ml-0.5 rounded bg-muted/40 px-1 text-[9px] font-mono">A</kbd>
        </Button>
        <div className="flex items-center rounded-md border bg-card/90 shadow-xs h-7 px-1 text-xs font-mono">
          <button
            type="button"
            className="px-1.5 py-0.5 hover:bg-muted rounded text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"
            onClick={cam.zoomOut}
            title="Zoom Out"
          >
            -
          </button>
          <span className="px-1 min-w-[38px] text-center text-[10px] select-none text-muted-foreground">
            {Math.round(cam.zoom * 100)}%
          </span>
          <button
            type="button"
            className="px-1.5 py-0.5 hover:bg-muted rounded text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"
            onClick={cam.zoomIn}
            title="Zoom In"
          >
            +
          </button>
        </div>
        <Button
          variant="outline"
          size="sm"
          className="h-7 text-xs gap-1 shadow-xs"
          onClick={() => cam.reset()}
          title="Fit Breadboard Trainer to Screen"
        >
          <Maximize2 className="size-3.5" />
          <span>Fit</span>
        </Button>
      </div>

      <svg
        ref={svgRef}
        viewBox={cam.viewBox}
        preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid meet"
        className="bg-card h-full w-full touch-none rounded-md border"
        onWheel={cam.onWheel}
        onPointerDown={(e) => {
          dragStartRef.current = { x: e.clientX, y: e.clientY };
          cam.handlePointerDown(e);
          cam.startPan(e.clientX, e.clientY);
        }}
        onPointerMove={(e) => {
          if (cam.handlePointerMove(e)) return;
          if (cam.movePan(e.clientX, e.clientY)) return;
          const p = cam.toView(e.clientX, e.clientY);
          setCursorPos(p);

          // Check Trainer Panel sockets first (magnetic snap radius 16px)
          const nearSocket = trainerSocketsList.find(
            (s) => Math.hypot(p.x - s.point.x, p.y - s.point.y) <= 16,
          );
          if (nearSocket) {
            setHoverSocket(nearSocket);
            setHover(null);
            return;
          }
          setHoverSocket(null);

          // Check Breadboard holes (magnetic snap radius 14px)
          const bh = holeAt(spec, p.x - BOARD_OFFSET_X, p.y - BOARD_OFFSET_Y);
          if (bh) {
            const hp = holePoint(bh);
            const dist = Math.hypot(p.x - (BOARD_OFFSET_X + hp.x), p.y - (BOARD_OFFSET_Y + hp.y));
            if (dist <= 14) {
              setHover(bh);
              return;
            }
          }
          setHover(null);
        }}
        onPointerUp={(e) => {
          cam.handlePointerUp(e);
          cam.endPan();
        }}
        onPointerLeave={() => {
          cam.endPan();
          setHover(null);
          setHoverSocket(null);
          setCursorPos(null);
        }}
        onContextMenu={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
        onClick={(e) => {
          if (
            dragStartRef.current &&
            Math.hypot(e.clientX - dragStartRef.current.x, e.clientY - dragStartRef.current.y) > 4
          ) {
            return;
          }
          if (hoverSocket) {
            if (hoverSocket.label === "CLK" && !clockNode) {
              const id = addNode({ kind: "clock", label: "CLK", state: 0, pos: { x: 0, y: 0 } });
              if (id) clickPin({ node: id, pin: "Y" });
            } else if (hoverSocket.label === "+5V" && !vccNode) {
              const id = addNode({ kind: "rail", rail: "vcc", label: "+5V", pos: { x: 0, y: 0 } });
              if (id) clickPin({ node: id, pin: "VCC" });
            } else if (hoverSocket.label === "GND" && !gndNode) {
              const id = addNode({ kind: "rail", rail: "gnd", label: "GND", pos: { x: 0, y: 0 } });
              if (id) clickPin({ node: id, pin: "GND" });
            } else {
              clickPin(hoverSocket.pin);
            }
          } else if (hover) {
            clickHole(hover);
          } else {
            setSelectedWireId(null);
            select([]);
            cancelWire();
          }
        }}
      >
        <defs>
          <pattern
            id="holes"
            width={PITCH}
            height={PITCH}
            patternUnits="userSpaceOnUse"
            x={BOARD_PAD}
            y={BOARD_PAD}
          >
            <rect
              x={PITCH / 2 - 3.5}
              y={PITCH / 2 - 3.5}
              width={7}
              height={7}
              rx={1.5}
              className="dark:fill-slate-950 fill-white dark:stroke-zinc-600 stroke-slate-400"
              strokeWidth={0.85}
            />
          </pattern>
        </defs>

        {/* =============================================================== */}
        {/* LAYER 1: Base Chassis & Breadboard Body                          */}
        {/* =============================================================== */}
        <g id="layer-chassis-and-board">
          {/* Digital Logic Trainer Board Chassis Base */}
          <rect
            width={CHASSIS_W}
            height={CHASSIS_H}
            rx={12}
            fill="#020617"
            stroke="#1e293b"
            strokeWidth={2}
          />

          {/* Chassis Metallic Hex Corner Screws */}
          {[
            { x: 16, y: 16 },
            { x: CHASSIS_W - 16, y: 16 },
            { x: 16, y: CHASSIS_H - 16 },
            { x: CHASSIS_W - 16, y: CHASSIS_H - 16 },
          ].map((pos, idx) => (
            <g key={idx} className="pointer-events-none">
              <circle cx={pos.x} cy={pos.y} r={6.5} fill="#334155" stroke="#1e293b" strokeWidth={1} />
              <circle cx={pos.x} cy={pos.y} r={4.5} fill="#475569" />
              <line x1={pos.x - 3} y1={pos.y - 3} x2={pos.x + 3} y2={pos.y + 3} stroke="#0f172a" strokeWidth={1} />
            </g>
          ))}

          {/* Chassis Brand Silk-Screen Title (Silver on Dark Chassis) */}
          <g className="pointer-events-none select-none">
            <text
              x={24}
              y={34}
              fill="#f8fafc"
              className="font-mono text-[13px] font-bold tracking-wider"
            >
              DIGITAL LOGIC TRAINER
            </text>
            <text
              x={24}
              y={50}
              fill="#cbd5e1"
              className="font-mono text-[9px] tracking-wide"
            >
              MODEL 74XX-TTL · 5V BENCH
            </text>
          </g>

          {/* Top-Left: DC Power Supply Module */}
          <g>
            <rect
              x={375}
              y={14}
              width={205}
              height={82}
              rx={6}
              fill="#090d16"
              stroke="#1e293b"
              strokeWidth={1}
            />
            {/* Header row */}
            <text
              x={387}
              y={29}
              fill="#f8fafc"
              className="font-mono text-[9px] font-bold pointer-events-none select-none"
            >
              DC POWER SUPPLY
            </text>
            {/* Status Power LED & ON Label in header */}
            <circle cx={528} cy={26} r={3.5} fill="#10b981" />
            <circle cx={528} cy={26} r={6} fill="#10b981" opacity={0.25} />
            <text
              x={537}
              y={29}
              fill="#34d399"
              className="font-mono text-[8px] font-bold pointer-events-none select-none"
            >
              +5V ON
            </text>
            <line
              x1={375}
              y1={36}
              x2={580}
              y2={36}
              stroke="#1e293b"
              strokeWidth={0.75}
            />
            {/* +5.0V VCC Red Socket */}
            <TrainerSocket
              cx={430}
              cy={60}
              label="+5.0V"
              sublabel="VCC"
              color="#ef4444"
              isArmed={pendingPin !== null && pendingPin.node === vccNode?.id && pendingPin.pin === "VCC"}
              onClick={handleVccClick}
            />
            {/* 0.0V GND Blue Socket */}
            <TrainerSocket
              cx={520}
              cy={60}
              label="0.0V"
              sublabel="GND"
              color="#3b82f6"
              isArmed={pendingPin !== null && pendingPin.node === gndNode?.id && pendingPin.pin === "GND"}
              onClick={handleGndClick}
            />
          </g>

          {/* Top-Center: Pulse / Clock Generator Module */}
          <g>
            <rect
              x={595}
              y={14}
              width={295}
              height={82}
              rx={6}
              fill="#090d16"
              stroke="#1e293b"
              strokeWidth={1}
            />
            <text
              x={605}
              y={28}
              fill="#f8fafc"
              className="font-mono text-[8.5px] font-bold pointer-events-none select-none"
            >
              PULSE / CLK GENERATOR
            </text>
            {/* Manual Single-Pulse Button */}
            <g
              id="trainer-pulse-btn"
              data-testid="trainer-pulse-btn"
              className="cursor-pointer group"
              onClick={handlePulse}
              transform="translate(605, 42)"
            >
              <rect
                width={48}
                height={26}
                rx={4}
                className="fill-primary/90 hover:fill-primary stroke-border"
                strokeWidth={1}
              />
              <text
                x={24}
                y={16.5}
                textAnchor="middle"
                fill="#ffffff"
                className="font-mono text-[9px] font-bold pointer-events-none select-none"
              >
                PULSE
              </text>
            </g>

            {/* Auto frequency buttons (1Hz, 2Hz, 5Hz) */}
            {[1, 2, 5].map((hzVal, i) => (
              <g
                key={hzVal}
                id={`trainer-clk-${hzVal}hz`}
                data-testid={`trainer-clk-${hzVal}hz`}
                className="cursor-pointer group"
                onClick={() => {
                  setAutoHz((curr) => {
                    if (curr === hzVal) return null;
                    const state = useCircuitStore.getState();
                    if (!Object.values(state.doc.nodes).some((n) => n.kind === "clock")) {
                      state.addNode({ kind: "clock", label: "CLK", state: 0, pos: { x: 0, y: 0 } });
                    }
                    return hzVal;
                  });
                }}
                transform={`translate(${660 + i * 36}, 42)`}
              >
                <rect
                  width={32}
                  height={26}
                  rx={4}
                  className={autoHz === hzVal ? "fill-primary" : "fill-slate-800 hover:fill-slate-700"}
                />
                <text
                  x={16}
                  y={16.5}
                  textAnchor="middle"
                  fill={autoHz === hzVal ? "#ffffff" : "#cbd5e1"}
                  className="font-mono text-[9px] font-bold pointer-events-none select-none"
                >
                  {hzVal}Hz
                </text>
              </g>
            ))}

            {/* Clock LED indicator */}
            <g
              className="cursor-pointer"
              onClick={(e) => {
                e.stopPropagation();
                const clk = clockNode;
                if (!clk) {
                  const id = addNode({ kind: "clock", label: "CLK", state: 0, pos: { x: 0, y: 0 } });
                  if (id) {
                    toggleClock(id);
                    return;
                  }
                } else {
                  toggleClock(clk.id);
                }
              }}
            >
              {clockNode?.state === 1 && (
                <circle
                  cx={788}
                  cy={55}
                  r={9}
                  fill="#f59e0b"
                  opacity={0.35}
                  className="pointer-events-none"
                />
              )}
              <circle
                cx={788}
                cy={55}
                r={5.5}
                fill={clockNode?.state === 1 ? "#f59e0b" : "#475569"}
              />
              <text
                x={788}
                y={70}
                textAnchor="middle"
                fill={clockNode?.state === 1 ? "#f59e0b" : "#94a3b8"}
                className="font-mono text-[7.5px] font-semibold pointer-events-none select-none"
              >
                {clockNode?.state === 1 ? "HIGH" : "LOW"}
              </text>
            </g>

            {/* CLK Socket */}
            <TrainerSocket
              cx={850}
              cy={55}
              label="CLK"
              sublabel="OUT"
              color="#f59e0b"
              isArmed={pendingPin !== null && pendingPin.node === clockNode?.id && pendingPin.pin === "Y"}
              onClick={handleClockClick}
            />
          </g>

          {/* Top-Right: Logic Indicators (Output Monitor LEDs) */}
          <g>
            <rect
              x={905}
              y={14}
              width={CHASSIS_W - 925}
              height={82}
              rx={6}
              fill="#090d16"
              stroke="#1e293b"
              strokeWidth={1}
            />
            {/* Header row */}
            <text
              x={918}
              y={29}
              fill="#f8fafc"
              className="font-mono text-[9px] font-bold pointer-events-none select-none"
            >
              LOGIC INDICATORS (OUTPUT MONITOR)
            </text>
            {/* Dedicated + LED Button in Top-Right Header */}
            <g
              className="cursor-pointer group"
              onClick={handleAddLed}
              transform={`translate(${CHASSIS_W - 82}, 18)`}
            >
              <rect
                width={54}
                height={20}
                rx={4}
                className="fill-slate-800 hover:fill-slate-700 stroke-slate-700 transition-colors"
                strokeWidth={1}
              />
              <text
                x={27}
                y={13.5}
                textAnchor="middle"
                fill="#f8fafc"
                className="font-mono text-[8.5px] font-semibold pointer-events-none select-none"
              >
                + LED
              </text>
            </g>
            <line
              x1={905}
              y1={36}
              x2={CHASSIS_W - 20}
              y2={36}
              stroke="#1e293b"
              strokeWidth={0.75}
            />
            {ledNodes.map((led, i) => (
              <TrainerLed
                key={led.id}
                led={led}
                cx={945 + i * 66}
                cy={52}
                isArmed={pendingPin !== null && pendingPin.node === led.id && pendingPin.pin === "A"}
                onClick={(e: React.MouseEvent) => {
                  e.stopPropagation();
                  clickPin({ node: led.id, pin: "A" });
                }}
                onColorChange={(color) => setLedColor(led.id, color)}
              />
            ))}
          </g>

          {/* Center: Solderless Breadboard Base & Body */}
          <g transform={`translate(${BOARD_OFFSET_X}, ${BOARD_OFFSET_Y})`}>
            <rect width={w} height={h} rx={6} className="dark:fill-zinc-900 fill-slate-100 dark:stroke-zinc-700 stroke-slate-300" strokeWidth={1} />
            <rect width={w} height={h} fill="transparent" />
            <BoardBody spec={spec} />
          </g>

          {/* Bottom: Logic Input Switches (Data Switches) */}
          <g>
            <rect
              x={60}
              y={595}
              width={w}
              height={88}
              rx={6}
              fill="#090d16"
